class PageNavigation{

  constructor(parents, tags){
    this.parent = 'stats';
    this.tag = 'all';
    this.parents = parents;
    this.tags = tags;
    this.targets = this.targets();
    this.find();
    this.needs_refresh = true;
  }

  // Filter Content Based on Parents / Tags

  filter(){
    let inst = this;

    this.targets.forEach(function(target){

      let show = false;

      if(target.parents.includes(inst.parent) && inst.tag == 'all'){show = true;}

      if(target.parents.includes(inst.parent) && target.tags && target.tags.includes('always')){show = true;}

      if(target.parents.includes(inst.parent) && target.tags && target.tags.includes(inst.tag)
      || target.parents.includes(inst.parent) && inst.tag == 'all'){
        show = true;
      }

      (show) ? target.el.style.display = "block" : target.el.style.display = "none";

    });

    if(this.needs_refresh){this.refresh();}

  }

  // Find Links to Filter from Initial HTML

  find(){
    let navigations = document.querySelectorAll('nav.sub-nav');
    let inst = this;
    navigations.forEach(nav =>{
      let links = nav.children;
      let type = nav.dataset.type;
      if(type == 'parent'){inst.main_nav = nav;}
      if(type == 'tag'){inst.sub_nav = {'el':nav, 'links':{}};}

      for (let i = 0; i < links.length; i++) {
        links[i].addEventListener('click', function(){
          if(type == 'parent'){
            if(inst.parent == links[i].dataset.uid){inst.needs_refresh = false;}else{inst.needs_refresh = true;}
            inst.parent = links[i].dataset.uid;
            if(inst.tags[inst.parent]){
              inst.tag = inst.tags[inst.parent][0];
            }else{
              inst.tag = 'all';
            }
            inst.is_active(inst.main_nav);
            inst.is_active(inst.sub_nav.el);
          }
          if(type == 'tag'){
            inst.needs_refresh = false;
            inst.tag = links[i].dataset.uid;
            inst.is_active(inst.sub_nav.el);
          }

          inst.filter();
        });
      }

    });

    this.generate_links();

  }

  // Find Content to Filter

  targets(){
    let targets = document.querySelectorAll('[data-parents]');
    let items = [];
    targets.forEach(target=>{
      items.push({
        'el':target,
        'parents':target.dataset.parents,
        'tags':target.dataset.tags || false,
      })
    });
    return items;
  }

  // Refresh Nav Based on Data

  refresh(){

    // Remove?
    (!this.tags[this.parent]) ? this.sub_nav.el.classList.add('closed') : this.sub_nav.el.classList.remove('closed');
    if(!this.tags[this.parent]){return;}

    if(this.sub_nav.links[this.parent]){
      this.sub_nav.el.innerHTML = "";
      this.sub_nav.links[this.parent].forEach(link=>{
        this.sub_nav.el.append(link);
      });
      this.is_active(this.sub_nav.el);
    }

  }

  // Generate Anchor Tags 

  generate_links(){
    let inst = this;
    for (const key in this.tags) {
      if (this.tags.hasOwnProperty(key)) {
        const links = this.tags[key];

        let temp = [];
        links.forEach(link=>{
          let item = document.createElement('a');
          item.innerHTML = link;
          item.dataset.uid = link;
          item.addEventListener('click',function(){
            inst.tag = item.dataset.uid;
            inst.needs_refresh = false;
            inst.is_active(inst.sub_nav.el);
            inst.filter();
          });
          temp.push(item);
        });

        inst.sub_nav.links[key] = temp;
      }
    }
  }

  // Is Active?

  is_active(nav){

    let links = nav.children;
    let type = nav.dataset.type;
    for (let i = 0; i < links.length; i++) {
      (this[type] == links[i].dataset.uid) ? links[i].classList.add('active') : links[i].classList.remove('active');
    }
    var top  = window.pageYOffset || document.documentElement.scrollTop;
    if (window.innerWidth < 800 && top > 262) {
       window.scrollTo(0, 262);
    }
    else if(window.innerWidth >= 800 && top > 204){
       window.scrollTo(0, 204);
    }
    
  }

}




class Dropdown{

  constructor(el){
    this.el = el;
    this.status = false;
    this.has_action = false;
    this.get_options(el);
    this.listener();
  }

  // Function

  open(){
    this.parent.classList.add('active');
    (this.options) ? this.options.style.display = "inherit" : '';
    this.status = true;
  }

  close(){
    this.parent.classList.remove('active');
    (this.options) ? this.options.style.display = "none" : '';
    this.status = false;
  }

  refresh(){
    this.value_node.innerHTML = this.selected;
  }

  // Init

  get_options(el){

    // Initial Values

    this.parent = el.parentNode;

    if(this.parent.classList.contains('external')){
      // console.log(this.parent);
      this.external = true;
      // console.log(this);
    }

    // Has Callback?
    if(this.parent.dataset.action){
      this.has_action = true;
      this.action_name = this.parent.dataset.action;
      this.action_target = this.parent.dataset.target;
      this.action_target = document.getElementById(this.parent.dataset.target);
    }

    let selected = this.parent.querySelectorAll("a");
    if (selected.length == 0) {
      selected = this.parent.querySelectorAll("button");
    }

    if(typeof selected[0] === 'undefined'){
      // if undefined, then do nothing. prvent script from crashing
    }else{
      this.selected = selected[0].innerHTML;
    }
    

    el.childNodes.forEach(el =>{
      if(el instanceof HTMLImageElement){return;}
      if(el.tagName == 'I'){return;}
      if(el.tagName == 'SPAN'){this.value_node = el}
    });

    // Save Dropdown Element

    let siblings = el.parentNode.childNodes;
    siblings.forEach(sibling=>{
      if(sibling.classList && sibling.classList.contains('dropdown-options')){
        this.options = sibling;
      }
    });

    this.options.childNodes.forEach(link=>{
      this.item_listener(link);
    });

  }

  listener(){

    let inst = this;
    this.el.addEventListener('click', function(){

      Event.dropdown_clicked(inst.el);
      (inst.status) ? inst.close() : inst.open();

    });
  }

  item_listener(link){
    let inst = this;
    link.addEventListener('click', function(){
      if(!inst.external){
        inst.selected = link.innerHTML;
        inst.refresh();
        if(inst.has_action && link.dataset.uid){
          inst.actions(inst.action_name, link.dataset.uid, inst.action_target);
        }
      };
      inst.close();
    });
  }


  // Actions (AJAX Request)

  actions(name,value,target){
    alert(`This action was ${name} and the value was ${value} and the target is #${target}. We will pass this to h2h_ajax.php and generate the new HTML.`);
  }

}

class SwitchFixture{
  constructor() {
    this.el = false;
    this.find();
  }

  get(){
    // Fetch Games w/ Ajax
  }

  // Find Element(s)

  find(){
    this.el = document.getElementById('fixture-dropdown-el') || false;
  }

  // Attatch Link Events

  link_events(els){

    let list = this.el;

    els.forEach(element => {

      if(element.dataset.uid && element.dataset.uid == 'fixture-dropdown-el'){
        element.addEventListener('click', function(){

          this.classList.toggle('active');

          if(list.classList.contains('on')){
            list.classList.remove('on');
          }else{
            list.classList.add('on');
          }
        });
      }
    });
  }

}

class Grid {

  constructor(el){
    this.el = el;
    this.sort_direction = "highest";
    this.sorted_id = false;
    this.cells = {};
    this.flat_cells = [];
    this.container = false;
    this.capture();
  }

  // Sort

  // Capture Cells

  capture(){

    let cells = this.el.querySelectorAll('.cell');
    let inst = this;
    cells.forEach(cell => {

      if(cell.dataset.uid){
        
        if(cell.parentNode && cell.parentNode.classList.contains('header')){
          cell.addEventListener('click', function(){
            inst.sort(cell.dataset.uid);
          })
        }

        if(!this.cells[cell.dataset.uid]){this.cells[cell.dataset.uid] = [];}
        this.cells[cell.dataset.uid].push(cell);
        this.flat_cells.push(cell);

      }

    });

    this.get_widths();

  }

  // Align

  get_widths(){
    for (const id in this.cells) {
      if (this.cells.hasOwnProperty(id)) {
        const arr = this.cells[id];

        let max_width = 0;

        arr.forEach(cell =>{
          if(cell.offsetWidth >= max_width){max_width = cell.offsetWidth;}
        });

        this.align(arr,max_width);

      }
    }
  }

  align(els,width){
    els.forEach(el =>{
      el.style.width = width + "px";
    });
  }

  // Sort

  sort_focus(){
    this.flat_cells.forEach(cell =>{
      if(cell.dataset.uid && cell.dataset.uid == this.sorted_id){
        cell.classList.add('sorted');
      }else{
        cell.classList.remove('sorted');
      }
    });
  }

  sort(uid){

    let sorted = this.cells[uid];
    this.sorted_id = uid;
    this.sort_direction = (this.sort_direction == 'highest') ? 'lowest' : 'highest';

    sorted = sorted.filter(function(cell){
      return !cell.parentNode.classList.contains('header');
    });

    sorted = sorted.sort((a, b) => Number(a.textContent) - Number(b.textContent));

    if(this.sort_direction == 'highest'){
      sorted.reverse();
    }

    sorted.forEach(cell=>{
      let row = cell.parentNode;
      this.el.removeChild(cell.parentNode);
      this.el.appendChild(row);
    });

    this.sort_focus();
  }

}

class Explainer{
  constructor(link,text){
    this.link = link;
    this.active = false;
    this.span = document.createElement('span');
    this.span.className = "explainer";
    this.span.innerHTML = text;
    this.create();
  }

  remove(){
    this.span.style.display = "none";
    this.link.classList.remove('active');
    this.active = false;
    Event.explainer_status(false);
  }

  create(){
    let inst = this;
    inst.link.addEventListener('click',function(e){
      if(!inst.active){
        inst.link.parentNode.insertBefore(inst.span, inst.link.nextSibling);
        inst.span.style.display = "block";
        inst.link.classList.add('active');
        inst.active = true;
        setTimeout(function(){ Event.explainer_status(inst); }, 500);
        return;
      }else{
        inst.span.style.display = "none";
        inst.link.classList.remove('active');
        inst.active = false;
        setTimeout(function(){ Event.explainer_status(inst); }, 500);
        return;
      }
    });
  }
}



var h2h = {
  switch_fixtures: false,
  widgets:[],
  parents:['stats','odds','lineups','predictions','fixtures','tables','tv'],
  dropdowns:[],
  grids:[],
  current_explainer:false,
  tags:{
    'stats':['all','goals','corners','cards','half','players'],
    'fixtures':['all','results','upcoming','H2H',],
  }, 
}

window.Event = new class{

  dropdown_clicked(clicked){
    h2h.dropdowns.forEach(function(dropdown){
      if(clicked == dropdown.el && dropdown.status){return;}
      dropdown.close();
    });
  }

  explainer_status(ex){h2h.current_explainer = ex;}

}

// Click Events

document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {

  for (var target = e.target; target && target != this; target = target.parentNode) {

    if(h2h.current_explainer
      && !e.target.matches('.explainer-link')
      && !e.target.matches('.explainer')){
      h2h.clear_explainer();
    }

      // if(target.matches('.toggle-element')){
      //   if(target.classList.contains('active')){return;}

      // }

  }
}, false);

document.querySelectorAll('.stat-table').forEach(table=>{

  h2h.grids.push(t);
});

h2h.switch_fixtures.link_events(h2h.dropdown_links);

document.querySelectorAll('.dropdown-link').forEach(link=>{

  h2h.dropdowns.push(dropdown);
});

  new Explainer(item, item.dataset.explainer);
})

  // console.log(h2h);



// H2H Basic Functions

  // Clear Explainers
  h2h.clear_explainer = function(){
    h2h.current_explainer.remove();
    return;
  }

<script>
	function setCookie2(name, value, days = 365) {
	  const expires = new Date(Date.now() + days * 864e5).toUTCString();
	  document.cookie = `${name}=${value}; expires=${expires}; path=/`;
	}

  $(document).ready(function() {
  	$('#contact-form-submit').on('click',function(e){
  		var original_text = $(this).text();
  		$(this).html("<p class='loadingAnim'><i style='font-size:14px !important;' class='far fa-futbol'></i> &nbsp;Sending... Takes a few moments...</p>");
  	});
  	function getCookie(name)
	{
	    var re = new RegExp(name + "=([^;]+)");
	    var value = re.exec(document.cookie);
	    return (value != null) ? unescape(value[1]) : null;
	}




  		// if user timezone is not set
  		var timezone_set = getCookie('tz');

  		  		if(timezone_set == "" || timezone_set == null){
		    $.ajax({
	            type: "POST",
	            url: "/ajax_pong.php",
	            data: 'timezone=' + jstz.determine().name(),
	            success: function(data){
	            	// instead of reloading page, change all of the current page's dates and then set the cookie?
	            	// Build the expiration date string:
					var expiration_date = new Date();
					var cookie_string = '';
					var timezone = jstz.determine().name();
					expiration_date.setFullYear(expiration_date.getFullYear() + 1);
					// Build the set-cookie string:
					cookie_string = "tz="+timezone+"; path=/; expires=" + expiration_date.toUTCString();
					// Create or update the cookie:
					document.cookie = cookie_string;
					// console.log(timezone);
					// Convert to 12:00 format
					var months = ['January','February','March','April','May','June','July','August','September','October','November','December'];
					var weekday=new Array(7);  //initialize weekday array for display later
					weekday[0]="Monday";
					weekday[1]="Tuesday";
					weekday[2]="Wednesday";
					weekday[3]="Thursday";
					weekday[4]="Friday";
					weekday[5]="Saturday";
					weekday[6]="Sunday";
					function addZero(i) {
				    	if (i < 10) {
					        i = "0" + i;
					    }
					    return i;
					}
					function pad(number, length){
					    var str = "" + number
					    while (str.length < length) {
					        str = '0'+str
					    }
					    return str
					}
					$(".timezone-convert-match-mobile,.timezone-convert-match-month,.timezone-convert-match-week,.timezone-convert-match-ampm,.timezone-convert-match-regular,.timezone-convert-match-h2h-neo,.timezone-convert-match-h2h").each(function(){
						var formatted_time = new Date(parseInt(this.dataset.time)*1000);
						var hours = formatted_time.getHours();
						var suffix = (hours >= 12)? 'pm' : 'am';
						var weekday_name = weekday[formatted_time.getDay()];
						weekday_name = weekday_name.substr(0,3);
						var month = formatted_time.getMonth();
						var month_name = months[formatted_time.getMonth()];
						var year = formatted_time.getFullYear();
						var day = formatted_time.getDate();
						//only -12 from hours if it is greater than 12 (if not back at mid night)
					    hours = (hours > 12)? hours -12 : hours;
					    //if 00 then it is 12 am
					    hours = (hours == '00')? 12 : hours;
					    var minutes = addZero(formatted_time.getMinutes());
					    if($(this).hasClass("timezone-convert-match-regular")){
					    	$(this).text(hours + ":" + minutes + suffix);
					    }
					    if($(this).hasClass("timezone-convert-match-mobile")){
					    	$(this).text(hours + ":" + minutes);
					    }
					    if($(this).hasClass("timezone-convert-match-ampm")){
					    	$(this).text(suffix);
					    }
					    if($(this).hasClass("timezone-convert-match-week")){
					    	$(this).text(weekday_name + " " + day + ", " + hours + ":" + minutes + suffix);
					    }
					    if($(this).hasClass("timezone-convert-match-month")){
					    	$(this).text(month_name + "/" + day + ", " + hours + ":" + minutes + suffix);
					    }
					    if($(this).hasClass("timezone-convert-match-h2h")){
					    	$(this).text(weekday_name + ", " + month_name + " " + day + year);
					    }
					    if($(this).hasClass("timezone-convert-match-h2h-neo")){
					    	$(this).text(month_name + " " + day + ", " + year + " - " + hours + ":" + minutes + suffix);
					    }
					});
	                // location.reload();
	            }
	        });	
		}
		
		var isMobile = false;
		

		// Set default cookie to 'off' on first visit
		if (getCookie('darkmode') === undefined) {
		  setCookie2('darkmode', 'off');
		}

		const darkModeToggle = document.getElementById('darkModeToggle');

		if (darkModeToggle) {

			darkModeToggle.addEventListener('click', () => {

			  if (getCookie('cartalyst_sentinel') === undefined || getCookie('cartalyst_sentinel') === null || getCookie('cartalyst_sentinel') == "null") {

			    // If not logged in (cookie missing), redirect to /register
			    window.location.href = '/register?z=darkmode';
			    return; // Prevent further code execution
			  }

			  if (getCookie('darkmode') === "on") {
			  	setCookie2('darkmode',  'off' );
			  }else{
			  	setCookie2('darkmode',  'on' );
			  }
			  
			  location.reload();

			});
		}


    });
